        - `RequiredBytes` (integer; format: int64): The volume must be at least this big. The value of 0 indicates an unspecified minimum
        - `LimitBytes` (integer; format: int64): The volume must not be bigger than this. The value of 0 indicates an unspecified maximum.
      - `Availability` (string; enum: `active`, `pause`, `drain`; default: `active`): The availability of the volume for use in tasks. - `active` The volume is fully available for scheduling on the cluster - `pause` No new workloads should use the volume, but existing workloads are not stopped. - `drain` All workloads using this volume should be stopped and rescheduled, and no new ones should be started.

**Responses**

- `200`: no error
- `400`: bad parameter
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `404`: no such volume
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `503`: node is not part of a swarm
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `DELETE /volumes/{name}`

**Remove a volume**

Instruct the driver to remove the volume.

**Parameters**

- `name` (path; required; string): Volume name or ID
- `force` (query; boolean; default: `false`): Force the removal of the volume

**Responses**

- `204`: The volume was removed
- `404`: No such volume or volume driver
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `409`: Volume is in use and cannot be removed
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: Server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /volumes/prune`

**Delete unused volumes**

**Parameters**

- `filters` (query; string): Filters to process on the prune list, encoded as JSON (a `map[string][]string`). Available filters: - `label` (`label=<key>`, `label=<key>=<value>`, `label!=<key>`, or `label!=<key>=<value>`) Prune volumes with (or without, in case `label!=...` is used) the specified labels. - `all` (`all=true`) - Consider all (local) volumes for pruning and not just anonymous volumes.

**Responses**

- `200`: No error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object)
    - `VolumesDeleted` (array): Volumes that were deleted
      - `items` (string)
    - `SpaceReclaimed` (integer; format: int64): Disk space reclaimed in bytes
- `500`: Server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `GET /networks`

**List networks**

Returns a list of networks. For details on the format, see the
network inspect endpoint.

Note that it uses a different, smaller representation of a network than
inspecting a single network. For example, the list of containers attached
to the network is not propagated in API versions 1.28 and up.

**Parameters**

- `filters` (query; string): JSON encoded value of the filters (a `map[string][]string`) to process on the networks list. Available filters: - `dangling=<boolean>` When set to `true` (or `1`), returns all networks that are not in use by a container. When set to `false` (or `0`), only networks that are in use by one or more containers are returned. - `driver=<driver-name>` Matches a network's driver. - `id=<network-id>` Matches all or part of a network ID. - `label=<key>` or `label=<key>=<value>` of a network label. - `name=<network-name>` Matches all or part of a network name. - `scope=["swarm"|"global"|"local"]` Filters networks by scope (`swarm`, `global`, or `local`). - `type=["custom"|"builtin"]` Filters networks by type. The `custom` keyword returns all user-defined networks.

**Responses**

- `200`: No error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (array)
    - `items` (object): Network list response item
      - allOf:
        - `variant 1` (object)
          - `Name` (string): Name of the network.
            - Example: `my_network`
          - `Id` (string): ID that uniquely identifies a network on a single machine.
            - Example: `7d86d31b1478e7cca9ebed7e73aa0fdeec46c5ca29497431d3007d2d9e15ed99`
          - `Created` (string; format: dateTime): Date and time at which the network was created in [RFC 3339](https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc3339.txt) format with nano-seconds.
            - Example: `2016-10-19T04:33:30.360899459Z`
          - `Scope` (string): The level at which the network exists (e.g. `swarm` for cluster-wide or `local` for machine level)
            - Example: `local`
          - `Driver` (string): The name of the driver used to create the network (e.g. `bridge`, `overlay`).
            - Example: `overlay`
          - `EnableIPv4` (boolean): Whether the network was created with IPv4 enabled.
            - Example: `true`
          - `EnableIPv6` (boolean): Whether the network was created with IPv6 enabled.
            - Example: `false`
          - `IPAM` (object)
            - `Driver` (string; default: `default`): Name of the IPAM driver to use.
              - Example: `default`
            - `Config` (array): List of IPAM configuration options, specified as a map: `{"Subnet": <CIDR>, "IPRange": <CIDR>, "Gateway": <IP address>, "AuxAddress": <device_name:IP address>}`
              - `items` (object)
                - `Subnet` (string)
                  - Example: `172.20.0.0/16`
                - `IPRange` (string)
                  - Example: `172.20.10.0/24`
                - `Gateway` (string)
                  - Example: `172.20.10.11`
                - `AuxiliaryAddresses` (object)
                  - `additional properties` (string)
            - `Options` (object): Driver-specific options, specified as a map.
              - Example: `{"foo":"bar"}`
              - `additional properties` (string)
          - `Internal` (boolean; default: `false`): Whether the network is created to only allow internal networking connectivity.
            - Example: `false`
          - `Attachable` (boolean; default: `false`): Whether a global / swarm scope network is manually attachable by regular containers from workers in swarm mode.
            - Example: `false`
          - `Ingress` (boolean; default: `false`): Whether the network is providing the routing-mesh for the swarm cluster.
            - Example: `false`
          - `ConfigFrom` (object): The config-only network source to provide the configuration for this network.
            - `Network` (string): The name of the config-only network that provides the network's configuration. The specified network must be an existing config-only network. Only network names are allowed, not network IDs.
              - Example: `config_only_network_01`
          - `ConfigOnly` (boolean; default: `false`): Whether the network is a config-only network. Config-only networks are placeholder networks for network configurations to be used by other networks. Config-only networks cannot be used directly to run containers or services.
          - `Options` (object): Network-specific options uses when creating the network.
            - Example: `{"com.docker.network.bridge.default_bridge":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.enable_icc":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.enable_ip_masquerade":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.host_binding_ipv4":"0.0.0.0","com.docker.network.bridge.name":"docker0","com.docker.network.driver.mtu":"1500"}`
            - `additional properties` (string)
          - `Labels` (object): Metadata specific to the network being created.
            - Example: `{"com.example.some-label":"some-value","com.example.some-other-label":"some-other-value"}`
            - `additional properties` (string)
          - `Peers` (array): List of peer nodes for an overlay network. This field is only present for overlay networks, and omitted for other network types.
            - `items` (object): represents one peer of an overlay network.
              - `Name` (string): ID of the peer-node in the Swarm cluster.
                - Example: `6869d7c1732b`
              - `IP` (string): IP-address of the peer-node in the Swarm cluster.
                - Example: `10.133.77.91`
  - Example `application/json`: `[{"Name":"bridge","Id":"f2de39df4171b0dc801e8002d1d999b77256983dfc63041c0f34030aa3977566","Created":"2016-10-19T06:21:00.416543526Z","Scope":"local","Driver":"bridge","EnableIPv4":true,"EnableIPv6":false,"Internal":false,"Attachable":false,"Ingress":false,"IPAM":{"Driver":"default","Config":[{"Subnet":"172.17.0.0/16"}]},"Options":{"com.docker.network.bridge.default_bridge":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.enable_icc":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.enable_ip_masquerade":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.host_binding_ipv4":"0.0.0.0","com.docker.network.bridge.name":"docker0","com.docker.network.driver.mtu":"1500"}},{"Name":"none","Id":"e086a3893b05ab69242d3c44e49483a3bbbd3a26b46baa8f61ab797c1088d794","Created":"0001-01-01T00:00:00Z","Scope":"local","Driver":"null","EnableIPv4":false,"EnableIPv6":false,"Internal":false,"Attachable":false,"Ingress":false,"IPAM":{"Driver":"default","Config":[]},"Containers":{},"Options":{}},{"Name":"host","Id":"13e871235c677f196c4e1ecebb9dc733b9b2d2ab589e30c539efeda84a24215e","Created":"0001-01-01T00:00:00Z","Scope":"local","Driver":"host","EnableIPv4":false,"EnableIPv6":false,"Internal":false,"Attachable":false,"Ingress":false,"IPAM":{"Driver":"default","Config":[]},"Containers":{},"Options":{}}]`
- `500`: Server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `GET /networks/{id}`

**Inspect a network**

**Parameters**

- `id` (path; required; string): Network ID or name
- `verbose` (query; boolean; default: `false`): Detailed inspect output for troubleshooting
- `scope` (query; string): Filter the network by scope (swarm, global, or local)

**Responses**

- `200`: No error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): The body of the "get network" http response message.
    - allOf:
      - `variant 1` (object)
        - `Name` (string): Name of the network.
          - Example: `my_network`
        - `Id` (string): ID that uniquely identifies a network on a single machine.
          - Example: `7d86d31b1478e7cca9ebed7e73aa0fdeec46c5ca29497431d3007d2d9e15ed99`
        - `Created` (string; format: dateTime): Date and time at which the network was created in [RFC 3339](https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc3339.txt) format with nano-seconds.
          - Example: `2016-10-19T04:33:30.360899459Z`
        - `Scope` (string): The level at which the network exists (e.g. `swarm` for cluster-wide or `local` for machine level)
          - Example: `local`
        - `Driver` (string): The name of the driver used to create the network (e.g. `bridge`, `overlay`).
          - Example: `overlay`
        - `EnableIPv4` (boolean): Whether the network was created with IPv4 enabled.
          - Example: `true`
        - `EnableIPv6` (boolean): Whether the network was created with IPv6 enabled.
          - Example: `false`
        - `IPAM` (object)
          - `Driver` (string; default: `default`): Name of the IPAM driver to use.
            - Example: `default`
          - `Config` (array): List of IPAM configuration options, specified as a map: `{"Subnet": <CIDR>, "IPRange": <CIDR>, "Gateway": <IP address>, "AuxAddress": <device_name:IP address>}`
            - `items` (object)
              - `Subnet` (string)
                - Example: `172.20.0.0/16`
              - `IPRange` (string)
                - Example: `172.20.10.0/24`
              - `Gateway` (string)
                - Example: `172.20.10.11`
              - `AuxiliaryAddresses` (object)
                - `additional properties` (string)
          - `Options` (object): Driver-specific options, specified as a map.
            - Example: `{"foo":"bar"}`
            - `additional properties` (string)
        - `Internal` (boolean; default: `false`): Whether the network is created to only allow internal networking connectivity.
          - Example: `false`
        - `Attachable` (boolean; default: `false`): Whether a global / swarm scope network is manually attachable by regular containers from workers in swarm mode.
          - Example: `false`
        - `Ingress` (boolean; default: `false`): Whether the network is providing the routing-mesh for the swarm cluster.
          - Example: `false`
        - `ConfigFrom` (object): The config-only network source to provide the configuration for this network.
          - `Network` (string): The name of the config-only network that provides the network's configuration. The specified network must be an existing config-only network. Only network names are allowed, not network IDs.
            - Example: `config_only_network_01`
        - `ConfigOnly` (boolean; default: `false`): Whether the network is a config-only network. Config-only networks are placeholder networks for network configurations to be used by other networks. Config-only networks cannot be used directly to run containers or services.
        - `Options` (object): Network-specific options uses when creating the network.
          - Example: `{"com.docker.network.bridge.default_bridge":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.enable_icc":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.enable_ip_masquerade":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.host_binding_ipv4":"0.0.0.0","com.docker.network.bridge.name":"docker0","com.docker.network.driver.mtu":"1500"}`
          - `additional properties` (string)
        - `Labels` (object): Metadata specific to the network being created.
          - Example: `{"com.example.some-label":"some-value","com.example.some-other-label":"some-other-value"}`
          - `additional properties` (string)
        - `Peers` (array): List of peer nodes for an overlay network. This field is only present for overlay networks, and omitted for other network types.
          - `items` (object): represents one peer of an overlay network.
            - `Name` (string): ID of the peer-node in the Swarm cluster.
              - Example: `6869d7c1732b`
            - `IP` (string): IP-address of the peer-node in the Swarm cluster.
              - Example: `10.133.77.91`
    - `Containers` (object): Contains endpoints attached to the network.
      - Example: `{"19a4d5d687db25203351ed79d478946f861258f018fe384f229f2efa4b23513c":{"Name":"test","EndpointID":"628cadb8bcb92de107b2a1e516cbffe463e321f548feb37697cce00ad694f21a","MacAddress":"02:42:ac:13:00:02","IPv4Address":"172.19.0.2/16","IPv6Address":""}}`
      - `additional properties` (object): contains network resources allocated and used for a container in a network.
        - `Name` (string)
          - Example: `container_1`
        - `EndpointID` (string)
          - Example: `628cadb8bcb92de107b2a1e516cbffe463e321f548feb37697cce00ad694f21a`
        - `MacAddress` (string)
          - Example: `02:42:ac:13:00:02`
        - `IPv4Address` (string)
          - Example: `172.19.0.2/16`
        - `IPv6Address` (string)
          - Example: \`\`
    - `Services` (object): List of services using the network. This field is only present for swarm scope networks, and omitted for local scope networks.
      - `additional properties`
    - `Status` (object): provides runtime information about the network such as the number of allocated IPs.
      - `IPAM` (object)
        - `Subnets` (object)
          - Example: `{"172.16.0.0/16":{"IPsInUse":3,"DynamicIPsAvailable":65533},"2001:db8:abcd:0012::0/96":{"IPsInUse":5,"DynamicIPsAvailable":4294967291}}`
          - `additional properties` (object)
            - `IPsInUse` (integer; format: uint64): Number of IP addresses in the subnet that are in use or reserved and are therefore unavailable for allocation, saturating at 2<sup>64</sup> - 1.
            - `DynamicIPsAvailable` (integer; format: uint64): Number of IP addresses within the network's IPRange for the subnet that are available for allocation, saturating at 2<sup>64</sup> - 1.
- `404`: Network not found
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: Server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `DELETE /networks/{id}`

**Remove a network**

**Parameters**

- `id` (path; required; string): Network ID or name

**Responses**

- `204`: No error
- `403`: operation not supported for pre-defined networks
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `404`: no such network
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: Server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /networks/create`

**Create a network**

**Request body** (required): Network configuration

- Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
- Schema (object)
  - `Name` (required; string): The network's name.
    - Example: `my_network`
  - `Driver` (string; default: `bridge`): Name of the network driver plugin to use.
    - Example: `bridge`
  - `Scope` (string): The level at which the network exists (e.g. `swarm` for cluster-wide or `local` for machine level).
  - `Internal` (boolean): Restrict external access to the network.
  - `Attachable` (boolean): Globally scoped network is manually attachable by regular containers from workers in swarm mode.
    - Example: `true`
  - `Ingress` (boolean): Ingress network is the network which provides the routing-mesh in swarm mode.
    - Example: `false`
  - `ConfigOnly` (boolean; default: `false`): Creates a config-only network. Config-only networks are placeholder networks for network configurations to be used by other networks. Config-only networks cannot be used directly to run containers or services.
    - Example: `false`
  - `ConfigFrom` (object): The config-only network source to provide the configuration for this network.
    - `Network` (string): The name of the config-only network that provides the network's configuration. The specified network must be an existing config-only network. Only network names are allowed, not network IDs.
      - Example: `config_only_network_01`
  - `IPAM` (object)
    - `Driver` (string; default: `default`): Name of the IPAM driver to use.
      - Example: `default`
    - `Config` (array): List of IPAM configuration options, specified as a map: `{"Subnet": <CIDR>, "IPRange": <CIDR>, "Gateway": <IP address>, "AuxAddress": <device_name:IP address>}`
      - `items` (object)
        - `Subnet` (string)
          - Example: `172.20.0.0/16`
        - `IPRange` (string)
          - Example: `172.20.10.0/24`
        - `Gateway` (string)
          - Example: `172.20.10.11`
        - `AuxiliaryAddresses` (object)
          - `additional properties` (string)
    - `Options` (object): Driver-specific options, specified as a map.
      - Example: `{"foo":"bar"}`
      - `additional properties` (string)
  - `EnableIPv4` (boolean): Enable IPv4 on the network.
    - Example: `true`
  - `EnableIPv6` (boolean): Enable IPv6 on the network.
    - Example: `true`
  - `Options` (object): Network specific options to be used by the drivers.
    - Example: `{"com.docker.network.bridge.default_bridge":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.enable_icc":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.enable_ip_masquerade":"true","com.docker.network.bridge.host_binding_ipv4":"0.0.0.0","com.docker.network.bridge.name":"docker0","com.docker.network.driver.mtu":"1500"}`
    - `additional properties` (string)
  - `Labels` (object): User-defined key/value metadata.
    - Example: `{"com.example.some-label":"some-value","com.example.some-other-label":"some-other-value"}`
    - `additional properties` (string)

**Responses**

- `201`: Network created successfully
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): OK response to NetworkCreate operation
    - `Id` (required; string): The ID of the created network.
      - Example: `b5c4fc71e8022147cd25de22b22173de4e3b170134117172eb595cb91b4e7e5d`
    - `Warning` (required; string): Warnings encountered when creating the container
      - Example: \`\`
- `400`: bad parameter
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `403`: Forbidden operation. This happens when trying to create a network named after a pre-defined network, or when trying to create an overlay network on a daemon which is not part of a Swarm cluster.
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `404`: plugin not found
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: Server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /networks/{id}/connect`

**Connect a container to a network**

The network must be either a local-scoped network or a swarm-scoped network with the `attachable` option set. A network cannot be re-attached to a running container

**Parameters**

- `id` (path; required; string): Network ID or name

**Request body** (required)

- Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
- Schema (object): NetworkConnectRequest represents the data to be used to connect a container to a network.
  - `Container` (required; string): The ID or name of the container to connect to the network.
    - Example: `3613f73ba0e4`
  - `EndpointConfig` (object): Configuration for a network endpoint.
    - `IPAMConfig` (object): EndpointIPAMConfig represents an endpoint's IPAM configuration.
      - `IPv4Address` (string)
        - Example: `172.20.30.33`
      - `IPv6Address` (string)
        - Example: `2001:db8:abcd::3033`
      - `LinkLocalIPs` (array)
        - Example: `["169.254.34.68","fe80::3468"]`
        - `items` (string)
    - `Links` (array)
      - Example: `["container_1","container_2"]`
      - `items` (string)
    - `MacAddress` (string): MAC address for the endpoint on this network. The network driver might ignore this parameter.
      - Example: `02:42:ac:11:00:04`
    - `Aliases` (array)
      - Example: `["server_x","server_y"]`
      - `items` (string)
    - `DriverOpts` (object): DriverOpts is a mapping of driver options and values. These options are passed directly to the driver and are driver specific.
      - Example: `{"com.example.some-label":"some-value","com.example.some-other-label":"some-other-value"}`
      - `additional properties` (string)
    - `GwPriority` (integer; format: int64): This property determines which endpoint will provide the default gateway for a container. The endpoint with the highest priority will be used. If multiple endpoints have the same priority, endpoints are lexicographically sorted based on their network name, and the one that sorts first is picked.
      - Example: `[10]`
    - `NetworkID` (string): Unique ID of the network.
      - Example: `08754567f1f40222263eab4102e1c733ae697e8e354aa9cd6e18d7402835292a`
    - `EndpointID` (string): Unique ID for the service endpoint in a Sandbox.
      - Example: `b88f5b905aabf2893f3cbc4ee42d1ea7980bbc0a92e2c8922b1e1795298afb0b`
    - `Gateway` (string): Gateway address for this network.
      - Example: `172.17.0.1`
    - `IPAddress` (string): IPv4 address.
      - Example: `172.17.0.4`
    - `IPPrefixLen` (integer): Mask length of the IPv4 address.
      - Example: `16`
    - `IPv6Gateway` (string): IPv6 gateway address.
      - Example: `2001:db8:2::100`
    - `GlobalIPv6Address` (string): Global IPv6 address.
      - Example: `2001:db8::5689`
    - `GlobalIPv6PrefixLen` (integer; format: int64): Mask length of the global IPv6 address.
      - Example: `64`
    - `DNSNames` (array): List of all DNS names an endpoint has on a specific network. This list is based on the container name, network aliases, container short ID, and hostname. These DNS names are non-fully qualified but can contain several dots. You can get fully qualified DNS names by appending `.<network-name>`. For instance, if container name is `my.ctr` and the network is named `testnet`, `DNSNames` will contain `my.ctr` and the FQDN will be `my.ctr.testnet`.
      - Example: `["foobar","server_x","server_y","my.ctr"]`
      - `items` (string)

**Responses**

- `200`: No error
- `400`: bad parameter
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `403`: Operation forbidden
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `404`: Network or container not found
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: Server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /networks/{id}/disconnect`

**Disconnect a container from a network**

**Parameters**

- `id` (path; required; string): Network ID or name

**Request body** (required)

- Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
- Schema (object): NetworkDisconnectRequest represents the data to be used to disconnect a container from a network.
  - `Container` (required; string): The ID or name of the container to disconnect from the network.
    - Example: `3613f73ba0e4`
  - `Force` (boolean; default: `false`): Force the container to disconnect from the network.
    - Example: `false`

**Responses**

- `200`: No error
- `403`: Operation not supported for swarm scoped networks
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `404`: Network or container not found
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: Server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /networks/prune`

**Delete unused networks**

**Parameters**

- `filters` (query; string): Filters to process on the prune list, encoded as JSON (a `map[string][]string`). Available filters: - `until=<timestamp>` Prune networks created before this timestamp. The `<timestamp>` can be Unix timestamps, date formatted timestamps, or Go duration strings (e.g. `10m`, `1h30m`) computed relative to the daemon machine’s time. - `label` (`label=<key>`, `label=<key>=<value>`, `label!=<key>`, or `label!=<key>=<value>`) Prune networks with (or without, in case `label!=...` is used) the specified labels.

**Responses**

- `200`: No error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object)
    - `NetworksDeleted` (array): Networks that were deleted
      - `items` (string)
- `500`: Server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `GET /plugins`

**List plugins**

Returns information about installed plugins.

**Parameters**

- `filters` (query; string): A JSON encoded value of the filters (a `map[string][]string`) to process on the plugin list. Available filters: - `capability=<capability name>` - `enable=<true>|<false>`

**Responses**

- `200`: No error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (array)
    - `items` (object): A plugin for the Engine API
      - `Id` (string)
        - Example: `5724e2c8652da337ab2eedd19fc6fc0ec908e4bd907c7421bf6a8dfc70c4c078`
      - `Name` (required; string)
        - Example: `tiborvass/sample-volume-plugin`
      - `Enabled` (required; boolean): True if the plugin is running. False if the plugin is not running, only installed.
        - Example: `true`
      - `Settings` (required; object): user-configurable settings for the plugin.
        - `Mounts` (required; array)
          - `items` (object)
            - `Name` (required; string)
              - Example: `some-mount`
            - `Description` (required; string)
              - Example: `This is a mount that's used by the plugin.`
            - `Settable` (required; array)
              - `items` (string)
            - `Source` (required; string)
              - Example: `/var/lib/docker/plugins/`
            - `Destination` (required; string)
              - Example: `/mnt/state`
            - `Type` (required; string)
              - Example: `bind`
            - `Options` (required; array)
              - Example: `["rbind","rw"]`
              - `items` (string)
        - `Env` (required; array)
          - Example: `["DEBUG=0"]`
          - `items` (string)
        - `Args` (required; array)
          - `items` (string)
        - `Devices` (required; array)
          - `items` (object)
            - `Name` (required; string)
            - `Description` (required; string)
            - `Settable` (required; array)
              - `items` (string)
            - `Path` (required; string)
              - Example: `/dev/fuse`
      - `PluginReference` (string): plugin remote reference used to push/pull the plugin
        - Example: `localhost:5000/tiborvass/sample-volume-plugin:latest`
      - `Config` (required; object): The config of a plugin.
        - `Description` (required; string)
          - Example: `A sample volume plugin for Docker`
        - `Documentation` (required; string)
          - Example: `https://docs.docker.com/engine/extend/plugins/`
        - `Interface` (required; object): The interface between Docker and the plugin
          - `Types` (required; array)
            - Example: `["docker.volumedriver/1.0"]`
            - `items` (string)
          - `Socket` (required; string)
            - Example: `plugins.sock`
          - `ProtocolScheme` (string; enum: \`\`, `moby.plugins.http/v1`): Protocol to use for clients connecting to the plugin.
            - Example: `some.protocol/v1.0`
        - `Entrypoint` (required; array)
          - Example: `["/usr/bin/sample-volume-plugin","/data"]`
          - `items` (string)
        - `WorkDir` (required; string)
          - Example: `/bin/`
        - `User` (object)
          - `UID` (integer; format: uint32)
            - Example: `1000`
          - `GID` (integer; format: uint32)
            - Example: `1000`
        - `Network` (required; object)
          - `Type` (required; string)
            - Example: `host`
        - `Linux` (required; object)
          - `Capabilities` (required; array)
            - Example: `["CAP_SYS_ADMIN","CAP_SYSLOG"]`
            - `items` (string)
          - `AllowAllDevices` (required; boolean)
            - Example: `false`
          - `Devices` (required; array)
            - `items` (object)
              - `Name` (required; string)
              - `Description` (required; string)
              - `Settable` (required; array)
                - `items` (string)
              - `Path` (required; string)
                - Example: `/dev/fuse`
        - `PropagatedMount` (required; string)
          - Example: `/mnt/volumes`
        - `IpcHost` (required; boolean)
          - Example: `false`
        - `PidHost` (required; boolean)
          - Example: `false`
        - `Mounts` (required; array)
          - `items` (object)
            - `Name` (required; string)
              - Example: `some-mount`
            - `Description` (required; string)
              - Example: `This is a mount that's used by the plugin.`
            - `Settable` (required; array)
              - `items` (string)
            - `Source` (required; string)
              - Example: `/var/lib/docker/plugins/`
            - `Destination` (required; string)
              - Example: `/mnt/state`
            - `Type` (required; string)
              - Example: `bind`
            - `Options` (required; array)
              - Example: `["rbind","rw"]`
              - `items` (string)
        - `Env` (required; array)
          - Example: `[{"Name":"DEBUG","Description":"If set, prints debug messages","Settable":null,"Value":"0"}]`
          - `items` (object)
            - `Name` (required; string)
            - `Description` (required; string)
            - `Settable` (required; array)
              - `items` (string)
            - `Value` (required; string)
        - `Args` (required; object)
          - `Name` (required; string)
            - Example: `args`
          - `Description` (required; string)
            - Example: `command line arguments`
          - `Settable` (required; array)
            - `items` (string)
          - `Value` (required; array)
            - `items` (string)
        - `rootfs` (object)
          - `type` (string)
            - Example: `layers`
          - `diff_ids` (array)
            - Example: `["sha256:675532206fbf3030b8458f88d6e26d4eb1577688a25efec97154c94e8b6b4887","sha256:e216a057b1cb1efc11f8a268f37ef62083e70b1b38323ba252e25ac88904a7e8"]`
            - `items` (string)

  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `GET /plugins/privileges`

**Get plugin privileges**

**Parameters**

- `remote` (query; required; string): The name of the plugin. The `:latest` tag is optional, and is the default if omitted.

**Responses**

- `200`: no error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (array)
    - Example: `[{"Name":"network","Description":"","Value":["host"]},{"Name":"mount","Description":"","Value":["/data"]},{"Name":"device","Description":"","Value":["/dev/cpu_dma_latency"]}]`
    - `items` (object): Describes a permission the user has to accept upon installing the plugin.
      - `Name` (string)
        - Example: `network`
      - `Description` (string)
      - `Value` (array)
        - Example: `["host"]`
        - `items` (string)
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /plugins/pull`

**Install a plugin**

Pulls and installs a plugin. After the plugin is installed, it can be
enabled using the `POST /plugins/{name}/enable` endpoint.

**Parameters**

- `remote` (query; required; string): Remote reference for plugin to install. The `:latest` tag is optional, and is used as the default if omitted.
- `name` (query; string): Local name for the pulled plugin. The `:latest` tag is optional, and is used as the default if omitted.
- `X-Registry-Auth` (header; string): A base64url-encoded auth configuration to use when pulling a plugin from a registry. Refer to the authentication section for details.

**Request body**

- Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
- Schema (array)
  - Example: `[{"Name":"network","Description":"","Value":["host"]},{"Name":"mount","Description":"","Value":["/data"]},{"Name":"device","Description":"","Value":["/dev/cpu_dma_latency"]}]`
  - `items` (object): Describes a permission the user has to accept upon installing the plugin.
    - `Name` (string)
      - Example: `network`
    - `Description` (string)
    - `Value` (array)
      - Example: `["host"]`
      - `items` (string)

**Responses**

- `204`: no error
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /plugins/{name}/enable`

**Enable a plugin**

**Parameters**

- `name` (path; required; string): The name of the plugin. The `:latest` tag is optional, and is the default if omitted.
- `timeout` (query; integer; default: `0`): Set the HTTP client timeout (in seconds)

**Responses**

- `200`: no error
- `404`: plugin is not installed
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /plugins/{name}/disable`

**Disable a plugin**

**Parameters**

- `name` (path; required; string): The name of the plugin. The `:latest` tag is optional, and is the default if omitted.
- `force` (query; boolean): Force disable a plugin even if still in use.

**Responses**

- `200`: no error
- `404`: plugin is not installed
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /plugins/{name}/upgrade`

**Upgrade a plugin**

**Parameters**

- `name` (path; required; string): The name of the plugin. The `:latest` tag is optional, and is the default if omitted.
- `remote` (query; required; string): Remote reference to upgrade to. The `:latest` tag is optional, and is used as the default if omitted.
- `X-Registry-Auth` (header; string): A base64url-encoded auth configuration to use when pulling a plugin from a registry. Refer to the authentication section for details.

**Request body**

- Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
- Schema (array)
  - Example: `[{"Name":"network","Description":"","Value":["host"]},{"Name":"mount","Description":"","Value":["/data"]},{"Name":"device","Description":"","Value":["/dev/cpu_dma_latency"]}]`
  - `items` (object): Describes a permission the user has to accept upon installing the plugin.
    - `Name` (string)
      - Example: `network`
    - `Description` (string)
    - `Value` (array)
      - Example: `["host"]`
      - `items` (string)

**Responses**

- `204`: no error
- `404`: plugin not installed
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /plugins/create`

**Create a plugin**

**Parameters**

- `name` (query; required; string): The name of the plugin. The `:latest` tag is optional, and is the default if omitted.

**Request body**: Path to tar containing plugin rootfs and manifest

- Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
- Schema (string; format: binary)

**Responses**

- `204`: no error
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

Push a plugin to the registry.

- `name` (path; required; string): The name of the plugin. The `:latest` tag is optional, and is the default if omitted.

**Responses**

- `404`: plugin not installed
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.

    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

  - Schema (object): Represents an error.

### `GET /nodes`

**List nodes**

**Parameters**

- `filters` (query; string): Filters to process on the nodes list, encoded as JSON (a `map[string][]string`). Available filters: - `id=<node id>` - `label=<engine label>` - `membership=`(`accepted`|`pending`)`-`name=<node name>`-`node.label=<node label>`-`role=`(`manager`|`worker`)`

**Responses**

- `200`: no error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (array)
    - `items` (object)
      - `ID` (string)
        - Example: `24ifsmvkjbyhk`
      - `Version` (object): The version number of the object such as node, service, etc. This is needed to avoid conflicting writes. The client must send the version number along with the modified specification when updating these objects. This approach ensures safe concurrency and determinism in that the change on the object may not be applied if the version number has changed from the last read. In other words, if two update requests specify the same base version, only one of the requests can succeed. As a result, two separate update requests that happen at the same time will not unintentionally overwrite each other.
        - `Index` (integer; format: uint64)
          - Example: `373531`
      - `CreatedAt` (string; format: dateTime): Date and time at which the node was added to the swarm in [RFC 3339](https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc3339.txt) format with nano-seconds.
        - Example: `2016-08-18T10:44:24.496525531Z`
      - `UpdatedAt` (string; format: dateTime): Date and time at which the node was last updated in [RFC 3339](https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc3339.txt) format with nano-seconds.
        - Example: `2017-08-09T07:09:37.632105588Z`
      - `Spec` (object)
        - Example: `{"Availability":"active","Name":"node-name","Role":"manager","Labels":{"foo":"bar"}}`
        - `Name` (string): Name for the node.
          - Example: `my-node`
        - `Labels` (object): User-defined key/value metadata.
          - `additional properties` (string)
        - `Role` (string; enum: `worker`, `manager`): Role of the node.
          - Example: `manager`
        - `Availability` (string; enum: `active`, `pause`, `drain`): Availability of the node.
          - Example: `active`
      - `Description` (object): NodeDescription encapsulates the properties of the Node as reported by the agent.
        - `Hostname` (string)
          - Example: `bf3067039e47`
        - `Platform` (object): Platform represents the platform (Arch/OS).
          - `Architecture` (string): Architecture represents the hardware architecture (for example, `x86_64`).
            - Example: `x86_64`
          - `OS` (string): OS represents the Operating System (for example, `linux` or `windows`).
            - Example: `linux`
        - `Resources` (object): An object describing the resources which can be advertised by a node and requested by a task.
          - `NanoCPUs` (integer; format: int64)
            - Example: `4000000000`
          - `MemoryBytes` (integer; format: int64)
            - Example: `8272408576`
          - `GenericResources` (array): User-defined resources can be either Integer resources (e.g, `SSD=3`) or String resources (e.g, `GPU=UUID1`).
            - Example: `[{"DiscreteResourceSpec":{"Kind":"SSD","Value":3}},{"NamedResourceSpec":{"Kind":"GPU","Value":"UUID1"}},{"NamedResourceSpec":{"Kind":"GPU","Value":"UUID2"}}]`
            - `items` (object)
              - `NamedResourceSpec` (object)
                - `Kind` (string)
                - `Value` (string)
              - `DiscreteResourceSpec` (object)
                - `Kind` (string)
                - `Value` (integer; format: int64)
        - `Engine` (object): EngineDescription provides information about an engine.
          - `EngineVersion` (string)
            - Example: `17.06.0`
          - `Labels` (object)
            - Example: `{"foo":"bar"}`
            - `additional properties` (string)
          - `Plugins` (array)
            - Example: `[{"Type":"Log","Name":"awslogs"},{"Type":"Log","Name":"fluentd"},{"Type":"Log","Name":"gcplogs"},{"Type":"Log","Name":"gelf"},{"Type":"Log","Name":"journald"},{"Type":"Log","Name":"json-file"},{"Type":"Log","Name":"splunk"},{"Type":"Log","Name":"syslog"},{"Type":"Network","Name":"bridge"},{"Type":"Network","Name":"host"},{"Type":"Network","Name":"ipvlan"},{"Type":"Network","Name":"macvlan"},{"Type":"Network","Name":"null"},{"Type":"Network","Name":"overlay"},{"Type":"Volume","Name":"local"},{"Type":"Volume","Name":"localhost:5000/vieux/sshfs:latest"},{"Type":"Volume","Name":"vieux/sshfs:latest"}]`
            - `items` (object)
              - `Type` (string)
              - `Name` (string)
        - `TLSInfo` (object): Information about the issuer of leaf TLS certificates and the trusted root CA certificate.
          - Example: `{"TrustRoot":"-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----\nMIIBajCCARCgAwIBAgIUbYqrLSOSQHoxD8CwG6Bi2PJi9c8wCgYIKoZIzj0EAwIw\nEzERMA8GA1UEAxMIc3dhcm0tY2EwHhcNMTcwNDI0MjE0MzAwWhcNMzcwNDE5MjE0\nMzAwWjATMREwDwYDVQQDEwhzd2FybS1jYTBZMBMGByqGSM49AgEGCCqGSM49AwEH\nA0IABJk/VyMPYdaqDXJb/VXh5n/1Yuv7iNrxV3Qb3l06XD46seovcDWs3IZNV1lf\n3Skyr0ofcchipoiHkXBODojJydSjQjBAMA4GA1UdDwEB/wQEAwIBBjAPBgNVHRMB\nAf8EBTADAQH/MB0GA1UdDgQWBBRUXxuRcnFjDfR/RIAUQab8ZV/n4jAKBggqhkjO\nPQQDAgNIADBFAiAy+JTe6Uc3KyLCMiqGl2GyWGQqQDEcO3/YG36x7om65AIhAJvz\npxv6zFeVEkAEEkqIYi0omA9+CjanB/6Bz4n1uw8H\n-----END CERTIFICATE-----\n","CertIssuerSubject":"MBMxETAPBgNVBAMTCHN3YXJtLWNh","CertIssuerPublicKey":"MFkwEwYHKoZIzj0CAQYIKoZIzj0DAQcDQgAEmT9XIw9h1qoNclv9VeHmf/Vi6/uI2vFXdBveXTpcPjqx6i9wNazchk1XWV/dKTKvSh9xyGKmiIeRcE4OiMnJ1A=="}`
          - `TrustRoot` (string): The root CA certificate(s) that are used to validate leaf TLS certificates.
          - `CertIssuerSubject` (string): The base64-url-safe-encoded raw subject bytes of the issuer.
          - `CertIssuerPublicKey` (string): The base64-url-safe-encoded raw public key bytes of the issuer.
      - `Status` (object): NodeStatus represents the status of a node. It provides the current status of the node, as seen by the manager.
        - `State` (string; enum: `unknown`, `down`, `ready`, `disconnected`): NodeState represents the state of a node.
          - Example: `ready`
        - `Message` (string)
          - Example: \`\`
        - `Addr` (string): IP address of the node.
          - Example: `172.17.0.2`
      - `ManagerStatus` (object): ManagerStatus represents the status of a manager. It provides the current status of a node's manager component, if the node is a manager.
        - `Leader` (boolean; default: `false`)
          - Example: `true`
        - `Reachability` (string; enum: `unknown`, `unreachable`, `reachable`): Reachability represents the reachability of a node.
          - Example: `reachable`
        - `Addr` (string): The IP address and port at which the manager is reachable.
          - Example: `10.0.0.46:2377`
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `503`: node is not part of a swarm
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `GET /swarm`

**Inspect swarm**

**Responses**

- `200`: no error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object)
    - allOf:
      - `variant 1` (object): ClusterInfo represents information about the swarm as is returned by the "/info" endpoint. Join-tokens are not included.
        - `ID` (string): The ID of the swarm.
          - Example: `abajmipo7b4xz5ip2nrla6b11`
        - `Version` (object): The version number of the object such as node, service, etc. This is needed to avoid conflicting writes. The client must send the version number along with the modified specification when updating these objects. This approach ensures safe concurrency and determinism in that the change on the object may not be applied if the version number has changed from the last read. In other words, if two update requests specify the same base version, only one of the requests can succeed. As a result, two separate update requests that happen at the same time will not unintentionally overwrite each other.
          - `Index` (integer; format: uint64)
            - Example: `373531`
        - `CreatedAt` (string; format: dateTime): Date and time at which the swarm was initialised in [RFC 3339](https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc3339.txt) format with nano-seconds.
          - Example: `2016-08-18T10:44:24.496525531Z`
        - `UpdatedAt` (string; format: dateTime): Date and time at which the swarm was last updated in [RFC 3339](https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc3339.txt) format with nano-seconds.
          - Example: `2017-08-09T07:09:37.632105588Z`
        - `Spec` (object): User modifiable swarm configuration.
          - `Name` (string): Name of the swarm.
            - Example: `default`
          - `Labels` (object): User-defined key/value metadata.
            - Example: `{"com.example.corp.type":"production","com.example.corp.department":"engineering"}`
            - `additional properties` (string)
          - `Orchestration` (object): Orchestration configuration.
            - `TaskHistoryRetentionLimit` (integer; format: int64): The number of historic tasks to keep per instance or node. If negative, never remove completed or failed tasks.
              - Example: `10`
          - `Raft` (object): Raft configuration.
            - `SnapshotInterval` (integer; format: uint64): The number of log entries between snapshots.
              - Example: `10000`
            - `KeepOldSnapshots` (integer; format: uint64): The number of snapshots to keep beyond the current snapshot.
            - `LogEntriesForSlowFollowers` (integer; format: uint64): The number of log entries to keep around to sync up slow followers after a snapshot is created.
              - Example: `500`
            - `ElectionTick` (integer): The number of ticks that a follower will wait for a message from the leader before becoming a candidate and starting an election. `ElectionTick` must be greater than `HeartbeatTick`. A tick currently defaults to one second, so these translate directly to seconds currently, but this is NOT guaranteed.
              - Example: `3`
            - `HeartbeatTick` (integer): The number of ticks between heartbeats. Every HeartbeatTick ticks, the leader will send a heartbeat to the followers. A tick currently defaults to one second, so these translate directly to seconds currently, but this is NOT guaranteed.
              - Example: `1`
          - `Dispatcher` (object): Dispatcher configuration.
            - `HeartbeatPeriod` (integer; format: int64): The delay for an agent to send a heartbeat to the dispatcher.
              - Example: `5000000000`
          - `CAConfig` (object): CA configuration.
            - `NodeCertExpiry` (integer; format: int64): The duration node certificates are issued for.
              - Example: `7776000000000000`
            - `ExternalCAs` (array): Configuration for forwarding signing requests to an external certificate authority.
              - `items` (object)
                - `Protocol` (string; enum: `cfssl`; default: `cfssl`): Protocol for communication with the external CA (currently only `cfssl` is supported).
                - `URL` (string): URL where certificate signing requests should be sent.
                - `Options` (object): An object with key/value pairs that are interpreted as protocol-specific options for the external CA driver.
                  - `additional properties` (string)
                - `CACert` (string): The root CA certificate (in PEM format) this external CA uses to issue TLS certificates (assumed to be to the current swarm root CA certificate if not provided).
            - `SigningCACert` (string): The desired signing CA certificate for all swarm node TLS leaf certificates, in PEM format.
            - `SigningCAKey` (string): The desired signing CA key for all swarm node TLS leaf certificates, in PEM format.
            - `ForceRotate` (integer; format: uint64): An integer whose purpose is to force swarm to generate a new signing CA certificate and key, if none have been specified in `SigningCACert` and `SigningCAKey`
          - `EncryptionConfig` (object): Parameters related to encryption-at-rest.
            - `AutoLockManagers` (boolean): If set, generate a key and use it to lock data stored on the managers.
              - Example: `false`
          - `TaskDefaults` (object): Defaults for creating tasks in this cluster.
            - `LogDriver` (object): The log driver to use for tasks created in the orchestrator if unspecified by a service. Updating this value only affects new tasks. Existing tasks continue to use their previously configured log driver until recreated.
              - `Name` (string): The log driver to use as a default for new tasks.
                - Example: `json-file`
              - `Options` (object): Driver-specific options for the selected log driver, specified as key/value pairs.
                - Example: `{"max-file":"10","max-size":"100m"}`
                - `additional properties` (string)
        - `TLSInfo` (object): Information about the issuer of leaf TLS certificates and the trusted root CA certificate.
          - Example: `{"TrustRoot":"-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----\nMIIBajCCARCgAwIBAgIUbYqrLSOSQHoxD8CwG6Bi2PJi9c8wCgYIKoZIzj0EAwIw\nEzERMA8GA1UEAxMIc3dhcm0tY2EwHhcNMTcwNDI0MjE0MzAwWhcNMzcwNDE5MjE0\nMzAwWjATMREwDwYDVQQDEwhzd2FybS1jYTBZMBMGByqGSM49AgEGCCqGSM49AwEH\nA0IABJk/VyMPYdaqDXJb/VXh5n/1Yuv7iNrxV3Qb3l06XD46seovcDWs3IZNV1lf\n3Skyr0ofcchipoiHkXBODojJydSjQjBAMA4GA1UdDwEB/wQEAwIBBjAPBgNVHRMB\nAf8EBTADAQH/MB0GA1UdDgQWBBRUXxuRcnFjDfR/RIAUQab8ZV/n4jAKBggqhkjO\nPQQDAgNIADBFAiAy+JTe6Uc3KyLCMiqGl2GyWGQqQDEcO3/YG36x7om65AIhAJvz\npxv6zFeVEkAEEkqIYi0omA9+CjanB/6Bz4n1uw8H\n-----END CERTIFICATE-----\n","CertIssuerSubject":"MBMxETAPBgNVBAMTCHN3YXJtLWNh","CertIssuerPublicKey":"MFkwEwYHKoZIzj0CAQYIKoZIzj0DAQcDQgAEmT9XIw9h1qoNclv9VeHmf/Vi6/uI2vFXdBveXTpcPjqx6i9wNazchk1XWV/dKTKvSh9xyGKmiIeRcE4OiMnJ1A=="}`
          - `TrustRoot` (string): The root CA certificate(s) that are used to validate leaf TLS certificates.
          - `CertIssuerSubject` (string): The base64-url-safe-encoded raw subject bytes of the issuer.
          - `CertIssuerPublicKey` (string): The base64-url-safe-encoded raw public key bytes of the issuer.
        - `RootRotationInProgress` (boolean): Whether there is currently a root CA rotation in progress for the swarm
          - Example: `false`
        - `DataPathPort` (integer; format: uint32; default: `4789`): DataPathPort specifies the data path port number for data traffic. Acceptable port range is 1024 to 49151. If no port is set or is set to 0, the default port (4789) is used.
          - Example: `4789`
        - `DefaultAddrPool` (array): Default Address Pool specifies default subnet pools for global scope networks.
          - `items` (string; format: CIDR)
            - Example: `["10.10.0.0/16","20.20.0.0/16"]`
        - `SubnetSize` (integer; format: uint32; default: `24`; maximum: `29`): SubnetSize specifies the subnet size of the networks created from the default subnet pool.
          - Example: `24`
      - `variant 2` (object)
        - `JoinTokens` (object): JoinTokens contains the tokens workers and managers need to join the swarm.
          - `Worker` (string): The token workers can use to join the swarm.
            - Example: `SWMTKN-1-3pu6hszjas19xyp7ghgosyx9k8atbfcr8p2is99znpy26u2lkl-1awxwuwd3z9j1z3puu7rcgdbx`
          - `Manager` (string): The token managers can use to join the swarm.
            - Example: `SWMTKN-1-3pu6hszjas19xyp7ghgosyx9k8atbfcr8p2is99znpy26u2lkl-7p73s1dx5in4tatdymyhg9hu2`
- `404`: no such swarm
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `503`: node is not part of a swarm
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /swarm/init`

**Initialize a new swarm**

**Request body** (required)

- Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
- Schema (object)
  - Example: `{"ListenAddr":"0.0.0.0:2377","AdvertiseAddr":"192.168.1.1:2377","DataPathPort":4789,"DefaultAddrPool":["10.10.0.0/8","20.20.0.0/8"],"SubnetSize":24,"ForceNewCluster":false,"Spec":{"Orchestration":{},"Raft":{},"Dispatcher":{},"CAConfig":{},"EncryptionConfig":{"AutoLockManagers":false}}}`
  - `ListenAddr` (string): Listen address used for inter-manager communication, as well as determining the networking interface used for the VXLAN Tunnel Endpoint (VTEP). This can either be an address/port combination in the form `192.168.1.1:4567`, or an interface followed by a port number, like `eth0:4567`. If the port number is omitted, the default swarm listening port is used.
  - `AdvertiseAddr` (string): Externally reachable address advertised to other nodes. This can either be an address/port combination in the form `192.168.1.1:4567`, or an interface followed by a port number, like `eth0:4567`. If the port number is omitted, the port number from the listen address is used. If `AdvertiseAddr` is not specified, it will be automatically detected when possible.
  - `DataPathAddr` (string): Address or interface to use for data path traffic (format: `<ip|interface>`), for example, `192.168.1.1`, or an interface, like `eth0`. If `DataPathAddr` is unspecified, the same address as `AdvertiseAddr` is used. The `DataPathAddr` specifies the address that global scope network drivers will publish towards other nodes in order to reach the containers running on this node. Using this parameter it is possible to separate the container data traffic from the management traffic of the cluster.
  - `DataPathPort` (integer; format: uint32): DataPathPort specifies the data path port number for data traffic. Acceptable port range is 1024 to 49151. if no port is set or is set to 0, default port 4789 will be used.
  - `DefaultAddrPool` (array): Default Address Pool specifies default subnet pools for global scope networks.
    - `items` (string)
      - Example: `["10.10.0.0/16","20.20.0.0/16"]`
  - `ForceNewCluster` (boolean): Force creation of a new swarm.
  - `SubnetSize` (integer; format: uint32): SubnetSize specifies the subnet size of the networks created from the default subnet pool.
  - `Spec` (object): User modifiable swarm configuration.
    - `Name` (string): Name of the swarm.
      - Example: `default`
    - `Labels` (object): User-defined key/value metadata.
      - Example: `{"com.example.corp.type":"production","com.example.corp.department":"engineering"}`
      - `additional properties` (string)
    - `Orchestration` (object): Orchestration configuration.
      - `TaskHistoryRetentionLimit` (integer; format: int64): The number of historic tasks to keep per instance or node. If negative, never remove completed or failed tasks.
        - Example: `10`
    - `Raft` (object): Raft configuration.
      - `SnapshotInterval` (integer; format: uint64): The number of log entries between snapshots.
        - Example: `10000`
      - `KeepOldSnapshots` (integer; format: uint64): The number of snapshots to keep beyond the current snapshot.
      - `LogEntriesForSlowFollowers` (integer; format: uint64): The number of log entries to keep around to sync up slow followers after a snapshot is created.
        - Example: `500`
      - `ElectionTick` (integer): The number of ticks that a follower will wait for a message from the leader before becoming a candidate and starting an election. `ElectionTick` must be greater than `HeartbeatTick`. A tick currently defaults to one second, so these translate directly to seconds currently, but this is NOT guaranteed.
        - Example: `3`
      - `HeartbeatTick` (integer): The number of ticks between heartbeats. Every HeartbeatTick ticks, the leader will send a heartbeat to the followers. A tick currently defaults to one second, so these translate directly to seconds currently, but this is NOT guaranteed.
        - Example: `1`
    - `Dispatcher` (object): Dispatcher configuration.
      - `HeartbeatPeriod` (integer; format: int64): The delay for an agent to send a heartbeat to the dispatcher.
        - Example: `5000000000`
    - `CAConfig` (object): CA configuration.
      - `NodeCertExpiry` (integer; format: int64): The duration node certificates are issued for.
        - Example: `7776000000000000`
      - `ExternalCAs` (array): Configuration for forwarding signing requests to an external certificate authority.
        - `items` (object)
          - `Protocol` (string; enum: `cfssl`; default: `cfssl`): Protocol for communication with the external CA (currently only `cfssl` is supported).
          - `URL` (string): URL where certificate signing requests should be sent.
          - `Options` (object): An object with key/value pairs that are interpreted as protocol-specific options for the external CA driver.
            - `additional properties` (string)
          - `CACert` (string): The root CA certificate (in PEM format) this external CA uses to issue TLS certificates (assumed to be to the current swarm root CA certificate if not provided).
      - `SigningCACert` (string): The desired signing CA certificate for all swarm node TLS leaf certificates, in PEM format.
      - `SigningCAKey` (string): The desired signing CA key for all swarm node TLS leaf certificates, in PEM format.
      - `ForceRotate` (integer; format: uint64): An integer whose purpose is to force swarm to generate a new signing CA certificate and key, if none have been specified in `SigningCACert` and `SigningCAKey`
    - `EncryptionConfig` (object): Parameters related to encryption-at-rest.
      - `AutoLockManagers` (boolean): If set, generate a key and use it to lock data stored on the managers.
        - Example: `false`
    - `TaskDefaults` (object): Defaults for creating tasks in this cluster.
      - `LogDriver` (object): The log driver to use for tasks created in the orchestrator if unspecified by a service. Updating this value only affects new tasks. Existing tasks continue to use their previously configured log driver until recreated.
        - `Name` (string): The log driver to use as a default for new tasks.
          - Example: `json-file`
        - `Options` (object): Driver-specific options for the selected log driver, specified as key/value pairs.
          - Example: `{"max-file":"10","max-size":"100m"}`
          - `additional properties` (string)

**Responses**

- `200`: no error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (string): The node ID
    - Example: `7v2t30z9blmxuhnyo6s4cpenp`
- `400`: bad parameter
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `503`: node is already part of a swarm
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /swarm/join`

**Join an existing swarm**

**Request body** (required)

- Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
- Schema (object)
  - Example: `{"ListenAddr":"0.0.0.0:2377","AdvertiseAddr":"192.168.1.1:2377","DataPathAddr":"192.168.1.1","RemoteAddrs":["node1:2377"],"JoinToken":"SWMTKN-1-3pu6hszjas19xyp7ghgosyx9k8atbfcr8p2is99znpy26u2lkl-7p73s1dx5in4tatdymyhg9hu2"}`
  - `ListenAddr` (required; string): Listen address used for inter-manager communication if the node gets promoted to manager, as well as determining the networking interface used for the VXLAN Tunnel Endpoint (VTEP). This is required for joining a swarm. If the port number is omitted, the default swarm listening port is used.
  - `AdvertiseAddr` (string): Externally reachable address advertised to other nodes. This can either be an address/port combination in the form `192.168.1.1:4567`, or an interface followed by a port number, like `eth0:4567`. If the port number is omitted, the port number from the listen address is used. If `AdvertiseAddr` is not specified, it will be automatically detected when possible.
  - `DataPathAddr` (string): Address or interface to use for data path traffic (format: `<ip|interface>`), for example, `192.168.1.1`, or an interface, like `eth0`. If `DataPathAddr` is unspecified, the same address as `AdvertiseAddr` is used. The `DataPathAddr` specifies the address that global scope network drivers will publish towards other nodes in order to reach the containers running on this node. Using this parameter it is possible to separate the container data traffic from the management traffic of the cluster.
  - `RemoteAddrs` (required; array): Addresses of manager nodes already participating in the swarm.
    - `items` (string)
  - `JoinToken` (required; string): Secret token for joining this swarm.

**Responses**

- `200`: no error
- `400`: bad parameter
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `500`: server error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.
- `503`: node is already part of a swarm
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.
    - Example: `{"message":"Something went wrong."}`
    - `message` (required; string): The error message.

### `POST /swarm/update`

**Update a swarm**

**Parameters**

- `version` (query; required; integer; format: int64): The version number of the swarm object being updated. This is required to avoid conflicting writes.
- `rotateWorkerToken` (query; boolean; default: `false`): Rotate the worker join token.
- `rotateManagerToken` (query; boolean; default: `false`): Rotate the manager join token.
- `rotateManagerUnlockKey` (query; boolean; default: `false`): Rotate the manager unlock key.

**Request body** (required)

- Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
- Schema (object): User modifiable swarm configuration.
  - `Name` (string): Name of the swarm.
    - Example: `default`
  - `Labels` (object): User-defined key/value metadata.
    - Example: `{"com.example.corp.type":"production","com.example.corp.department":"engineering"}`
    - `additional properties` (string)
  - `Orchestration` (object): Orchestration configuration.
    - `TaskHistoryRetentionLimit` (integer; format: int64): The number of historic tasks to keep per instance or node. If negative, never remove completed or failed tasks.
      - Example: `10`
  - `Raft` (object): Raft configuration.
    - `SnapshotInterval` (integer; format: uint64): The number of log entries between snapshots.
      - Example: `10000`
    - `KeepOldSnapshots` (integer; format: uint64): The number of snapshots to keep beyond the current snapshot.
    - `LogEntriesForSlowFollowers` (integer; format: uint64): The number of log entries to keep around to sync up slow followers after a snapshot is created.
      - Example: `500`
    - `ElectionTick` (integer): The number of ticks that a follower will wait for a message from the leader before becoming a candidate and starting an election. `ElectionTick` must be greater than `HeartbeatTick`. A tick currently defaults to one second, so these translate directly to seconds currently, but this is NOT guaranteed.
      - Example: `3`
    - `HeartbeatTick` (integer): The number of ticks between heartbeats. Every HeartbeatTick ticks, the leader will send a heartbeat to the followers. A tick currently defaults to one second, so these translate directly to seconds currently, but this is NOT guaranteed.
      - Example: `1`
  - `Dispatcher` (object): Dispatcher configuration.
    - `HeartbeatPeriod` (integer; format: int64): The delay for an agent to send a heartbeat to the dispatcher.
      - Example: `5000000000`
  - `CAConfig` (object): CA configuration.
    - `NodeCertExpiry` (integer; format: int64): The duration node certificates are issued for.
      - Example: `7776000000000000`
    - `ExternalCAs` (array): Configuration for forwarding signing requests to an external certificate authority.
      - `items` (object)
        - `Protocol` (string; enum: `cfssl`; default: `cfssl`): Protocol for communication with the external CA (currently only `cfssl` is supported).
        - `URL` (string): URL where certificate signing requests should be sent.
        - `Options` (object): An object with key/value pairs that are interpreted as protocol-specific options for the external CA driver.
          - `additional properties` (string)
        - `CACert` (string): The root CA certificate (in PEM format) this external CA uses to issue TLS certificates (assumed to be to the current swarm root CA certificate if not provided).
    - `SigningCACert` (string): The desired signing CA certificate for all swarm node TLS leaf certificates, in PEM format.
    - `SigningCAKey` (string): The desired signing CA key for all swarm node TLS leaf certificates, in PEM format.
    - `ForceRotate` (integer; format: uint64): An integer whose purpose is to force swarm to generate a new signing CA certificate and key, if none have been specified in `SigningCACert` and `SigningCAKey`
  - `EncryptionConfig` (object): Parameters related to encryption-at-rest.
    - `AutoLockManagers` (boolean): If set, generate a key and use it to lock data stored on the managers.
      - Example: `false`
  - `TaskDefaults` (object): Defaults for creating tasks in this cluster.
    - `LogDriver` (object): The log driver to use for tasks created in the orchestrator if unspecified by a service. Updating this value only affects new tasks. Existing tasks continue to use their previously configured log driver until recreated.
      - `Name` (string): The log driver to use as a default for new tasks.
        - Example: `json-file`
      - `Options` (object): Driver-specific options for the selected log driver, specified as key/value pairs.
        - Example: `{"max-file":"10","max-size":"100m"}`
        - `additional properties` (string)

**Responses**

- `200`: no error
- `400`: bad parameter
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (object): Represents an error.

### `GET /services`

**List services**

**Parameters**

- `filters` (query; string): A JSON encoded value of the filters (a `map[string][]string`) to process on the services list. Available filters: - `id=<service id>` - `label=<service label>` - `mode=["replicated"|"global"]` - `name=<service name>`
- `status` (query; boolean): Include service status, with count of running and desired tasks.

**Responses**

- `200`: no error
  - Media types: `application/json`, `text/plain`
  - Schema (array)
    - `items` (object)
      - Example: `{"ID":"9mnpnzenvg8p8tdbtq4wvbkcz","Version":{"Index":19},"CreatedAt":"2016-06-07T21:05:51.880065305Z","UpdatedAt":"2016-06-07T21:07:29.962229872Z","Spec":{"Name":"hopeful_cori","TaskTemplate":{"ContainerSpec":{"Image":"redis"},"Resources":{"Limits":{},"Reservations":{}},"RestartPolicy":{"Condition":"any","MaxAttempts":0},"Placement":{},"ForceUpdate":0},"Mode":{"Replicated":{"Replicas":1}},"UpdateConfig":{"Parallelism":1,"Delay":1000000000,"FailureAction":"pause","Monitor":15000000000,"MaxFailureRatio":0.15},"RollbackConfig":{"Parallelism":1,"Delay":1000000000,"FailureAction":"pause","Monitor":15000000000,"MaxFailureRatio":0.15},"EndpointSpec":{"Mode":"vip","Ports":[{"Protocol":"tcp","TargetPort":6379,"PublishedPort":30001}]}},"Endpoint":{"Spec":{"Mode":"vip","Ports":[{"Protocol":"tcp","TargetPort":6379,"PublishedPort":30001}]},"Ports":[{"Protocol":"tcp","TargetPort":6379,"PublishedPort":30001}],"VirtualIPs":[{"NetworkID":"4qvuz4ko70xaltuqbt8956gd1","Addr":"10.255.0.2/16"},{"NetworkID":"4qvuz4ko70xaltuqbt8956gd1","Addr":"10.255.0.3/16"}]}}`
      - `ID` (string)
      - `Version` (object): The version number of the object such as node, service, etc. This is needed to avoid conflicting writes. The client must send the version number along with the modified specification when updating these objects. This approach ensures safe concurrency and determinism in that the change on the object may not be applied if the version number has changed from the last read. In other words, if two update requests specify the same base version, only one of the requests can succeed. As a result, two separate update requests that happen at the same time will not unintentionally overwrite each other.
        - `Index` (integer; format: uint64)
          - Example: `373531`
      - `CreatedAt` (string; format: dateTime)
      - `UpdatedAt` (string; format: dateTime)
      - `Spec` (object): User modifiable configuration for a service.
        - `Name` (string): Name of the service.
        - `Labels` (object): User-defined key/value metadata.
          - `additional properties` (string)
        - `TaskTemplate` (object): User modifiable task configuration.
          - `PluginSpec` (object): Plugin spec for the service. *(Experimental release only.)* <p><br /></p> > **Note**: ContainerSpec, NetworkAttachmentSpec, and PluginSpec are > mutually exclusive. PluginSpec is only used when the Runtime field > is set to `plugin`. NetworkAttachmentSpec is used when the Runtime > field is set to `attachment`.
            - `Name` (string): The name or 'alias' to use for the plugin.
            - `Remote` (string): The plugin image reference to use.
            - `Disabled` (boolean): Disable the plugin once scheduled.
            - `PluginPrivilege` (array)
              - `items` (object): Describes a permission the user has to accept upon installing the plugin.
                - `Name` (string)
                  - Example: `network`
                - `Description` (string)
                - `Value` (array)
                  - Example: `["host"]`
                  - `items` (string)
          - `ContainerSpec` (object): Container spec for the service. <p><br /></p> > **Note**: ContainerSpec, NetworkAttachmentSpec, and PluginSpec are > mutually exclusive. PluginSpec is only used when the Runtime field > is set to `plugin`. NetworkAttachmentSpec is used when the Runtime > field is set to `attachment`.
            - `Image` (string): The image name to use for the container
            - `Labels` (object): User-defined key/value data.
              - `additional properties` (string)
            - `Command` (array): The command to be run in the image.
              - `items` (string)
            - `Args` (array): Arguments to the command.
              - `items` (string)
            - `Hostname` (string): The hostname to use for the container, as a valid [RFC 1123](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc1123) hostname.
            - `Env` (array): A list of environment variables in the form `VAR=value`.
              - `items` (string)
            - `Dir` (string): The working directory for commands to run in.
            - `User` (string): The user inside the container.
            - `Groups` (array): A list of additional groups that the container process will run as.
              - `items` (string)
            - `Privileges` (object): Security options for the container
              - `CredentialSpec` (object): CredentialSpec for managed service account (Windows only)
                - `Config` (string): Load credential spec from a Swarm Config with the given ID. The specified config must also be present in the Configs field with the Runtime property set. <p><br /></p> > **Note**: `CredentialSpec.File`, `CredentialSpec.Registry`, > and `CredentialSpec.Config` are mutually exclusive.
                  - Example: `0bt9dmxjvjiqermk6xrop3ekq`
                - `File` (string): Load credential spec from this file. The file is read by the daemon, and must be present in the `CredentialSpecs` subdirectory in the docker data directory, which defaults to `C:\ProgramData\Docker\` on Windows. For example, specifying `spec.json` loads `C:\ProgramData\Docker\CredentialSpecs\spec.json`. <p><br /></p> > **Note**: `CredentialSpec.File`, `CredentialSpec.Registry`, > and `CredentialSpec.Config` are mutually exclusive.
                  - Example: `spec.json`
                - `Registry` (string): Load credential spec from this value in the Windows registry.
